Image edit preview follow-up: free rotation and resize/resample now use bottom-right panels with transparent backdrops and preview on the main image. Rotation retains the current scale/pan, matching Alt drag; resize fits the requested aspect/size. Geometry reuses the current texture and preserves source/crop sampling bands, selection, view and edit history. There is no duplicate panel image. Cancel restores the prior presentation; Apply holds the preview through the existing resampling worker and adds one undoable edit. These are placement/size previews: final rotation pixels and the chosen resize filter are computed on Apply, explicitly stated in each panel. Other visual image edits already operate directly on the media; metadata/audio options need no counterpart. The first run passed 41 tests with two new failures: an incorrect four-vertex assumption in a mesh test and a real resize-preview sampling-band mismatch. The corrected 16-vertex assertion and separate display size pass all 44 affected regressions with one ignored opt-in, including compact clients, 100/125/200%, discarded passes, edited source UVs, Cancel, held Apply and Undo. One explicit hidden D3D11 reference test also passes with 45 readbacks; resize/rotation remain undimmed and the error modal retains its backdrop. The two 100% panel readbacks were visually inspected. This is automated/synthetic input and hidden rendering, not physical-input or desktop appearance approval. App all-target Release Clippy, fmt/diff/local links and the normal alternate Release pass (only the two expected output-name warnings). All 444 source/configuration hashes match; the only change after the 44-test run is the separately verified GPU reference expectation. Evidence: `target/tmp/image-modal-preview-results.json`; review: `target/release/start-image-modal-preview.cmd`. No installed-app replacement or push.

Filmstrip file-operation follow-up: preserve the open overlay and its bounded scroll offset while replacement Shell order is pending and after rename/move/delete, including moving the displayed file into another folder. Update retained tab view paths, retire operated-card focus instead of scrolling to its new index, and discard mutation/deleted-view transient state during tab view transfer. Ordinary external order updates keep their existing focus-following behavior. Only the deleted document's Filmstrip view inserts a `(deleted)` held entry among surviving real neighbors; the shared Shell listing excludes it. Cache that merged view by document owner/folder generation, suppress nonexistent-source preview/file/new-tab/window/drag routes, and retain normal primary dismissal and neighboring media navigation. Four density/current-versus-inactive mutation scenarios use real owned filesystem changes and injected completion snapshots, including nonzero-scroll deletion and cache reuse/order invalidation. A real loaded-image control verifies the held label at 100/125/200%, disabled new-owner routes, close/reopen and accessibility navigation to a surviving file, which releases the old document. The first related run passes 54 tests with six ignored opt-ins. The full app baseline passes 778 tests with one new fixture failure, 37 ignored and the owner-deferred picker excluded: removing an earlier row made the test accidentally choose the current file as its purported inactive deletion. The corrected selection and final transfer-state reset pass all 87 affected regressions, including Save, retained originals and tab transfer. One separately enabled owned native-Shell recycle test passes, preserving current media/edits/overlay and refreshing actual membership; app examples pass four tests with one opt-in ignored. This is a broad app baseline plus final affected rerun, not a new full-workspace aggregate or physical-input/visible-window approval. App all-target Release Clippy, fmt/diff/local links and the normal alternate Release pass, with only the two expected output-name warnings. All 444 source/configuration hashes match the final verified tree. Evidence: `target/tmp/filmstrip-mutations-results.json`; review: `target/release/start-filmstrip-mutations.cmd`. No installed-app replacement or push.

ICC speed gate: the test-only native WIC probe uses installed Adobe RGB / Japan Color 2001 Coated / sRGB profiles, generated RGBA/CMYK8 input, preallocated RGBA8 output, alternating unmanaged/managed copies and 8/24 fresh row workers on a Ryzen 9 9900X. One explicit Release opt-in passes in two independent final-process runs across thumbnail, 2.07 MP, 6 MP and 24 MP cases. It checks nonidentity conversion, exact sRGB identity, unchanged alpha and identical full pixels across both worker counts. For 24 MP, Adobe RGB conversion takes 181.8-182.4 ms with a reused transform, 31.1-31.4 ms including fresh 8-worker setup, and 19.4-21.7 ms with 24 workers. CMYK takes 242.7-246.4 / 49.7-51.1 / 27.8-28.0 ms respectively; unmanaged copies are about 4 ms. These are conversion probes, not end-to-end viewer latency, an IrfanView benchmark or evidence for every profile/optimized backend. The meaningful cost resolves the owner's gate in favor of stopping product integration. The supplied IrfanView analysis and LCMS_core decompilation show optional ICC handling, LittleCMS input/output profiles, per-image scanline workers and a wait for all workers, not free conversion or adjacent-image prefetch. Recommendation: retain current fast display for now; any later color-managed mode should avoid unnecessary transforms in an sRGB display path, cache converted results, bound worker use and respect Windows Advanced Color instead of blindly applying a monitor ICC. No display/OS-profile changes or new dependency. The only Rust change is an ignored cfg(test) probe; affected-package all-target Release Clippy, fmt/diff and local links pass. The first Clippy run required a documented allowance for its deliberate Debug-invocation guard. The full production baseline remains the 1,437-test loudness checkpoint below; no new full-suite or product Release aggregate is claimed. Evidence and profile/source/tool hashes: `target/tmp/icc-display-results.json`; full output: `target/tmp/icc-cost-final-a.log` and `target/tmp/icc-cost-final-b.log`.

Earlier checkpoints in this new goal are consolidated here; each has passing app all-target Release Clippy, fmt/diff/local-link checks and an alternate normal Release. Counts are per checkpoint, not an additive full-suite total. Generated readbacks and synthetic input are not physical approval; earlier initial failures and full test/artifact hashes remain in the named manifests and Git.

Windows font fallback: regular installed Windows language faces now follow the existing proportional/monospace chains, preserving existing Latin/Japanese metrics and the dedicated icon/reading-arrow families. Definitions and immutable bytes are shared once per process across windows; no font asset or dependency is added. Local font inspection confirms missing Hangul in the prior Figtree/Yu Gothic faces and coverage in Malgun Gothic. Four focused font tests pass, including missing/unreadable candidate handling, representative glyph atlas coverage for 11 installed families in two contexts, unchanged metrics and shared-byte identity. The explicit hidden GPU control passes with 27 readbacks at 100/125/200%; 100% and 200% multilingual tab/status images were inspected. Coverage depends on installed fonts; this does not certify complex-script shaping, every language or physical desktop appearance. The broad checkpoint has **1,397 passing tests, 117 ignored opt-ins and one owner-deferred picker exclusion**. The first app sweep found one stale expectation that upward dragging opens the audio timeline; a test-only correction now checks retained viewing mode followed by explicit timeline entry, and its native audio control passes. Other passed app tests were retained; core/runtime/integration/examples ran separately, so this is a combined verified checkpoint, not one uninterrupted green full-suite run. No capability SKIP was reported. Workspace all-target Release Clippy, fmt/diff/local-link checks and an alternate normal Release pass, with only expected `-o` warnings. Initial failure and final logs, source/font-coverage/readback/artifact hashes: `target/tmp/windows-fonts-results.json`; launcher: `target/release/start-windows-fonts.cmd`. Debug installation measurements are not Release startup or peak-memory benchmarks.

Custom modals: all nine modal entry points now share a floating frame, ordinary-sized white left-aligned heading, fixed action rows and a scrolling body that grows up to the current media viewport. The shared layout reserves wrapped button rows, releases outer vertical gaps in tiny clients, and resets egui Area's remembered maximum before body layout; raising only ScrollArea's limit had left long dialogs unnecessarily short. Body clipping excludes header/footer, and its scrollbar uses the full body width. Video previews keep their transparent backdrop and bottom-right placement. Other backdrops cover the full client independently of dialog placement; initial GPU review caught and corrected a clipped-backdrop band. **49 final affected passes / zero failures or skips**, including 48 input/layout/native controls and one explicit hidden GPU test with 36 full-client readbacks at 100/125/200%. Fixed header/footer, actual wheel movement, short-content shrink, long-content growth and media bounds are checked down to 240x150, with wrapped actions. Existing tests cover focus, keyboard/IME/popup Escape, transactional audio/metadata options, guards and actual native video Apply/Undo/Redo/export. The old wheel test now targets the scrollable filter instead of the fixed Cancel button. Final 100% resize/rotation and 200% long-error images were inspected. One unrelated keyboard-settings GPU opt-in remains ignored; the owner-deferred picker is untouched. App all-target Release Clippy, fmt/diff/local-link checks and alternate normal Release pass, with only expected `-o` warnings. Initial compile/test/geometry failures, final logs and source/artifact/readback hashes: `target/tmp/modal-layout-results.json`; launcher: `target/release/start-modal-layout.cmd`. This is automated and native offscreen evidence, not physical-operation approval or a new full-workspace aggregate.

Thumbnail context menus: Gallery and filmstrip now offer Open, Open in new window/tab, Copy file path and Reveal in File Explorer; Gallery adds Remove from history and filmstrip adds Rename/Move/Delete file. Open converts Gallery or replaces the current filmstrip view; new tabs remain in the background. Intents retain the exact clicked path and originating tab/media instance, rejecting stale or hidden targets. File preparation separates the displayed origin from the clicked target; owned native rename/move/recycle trials preserve the unrelated current tab, view and edits and verify refreshed Shell membership. Shared keyboard context entry, arrow navigation, Escape focus return and pointer actions are covered. **73 distinct affected passes**, including one explicit native recycle and one hidden GPU test with 42 full-client readbacks at 100/125/200%; both menus inspected at 100% and 200%. The initial Shell-refresh timeout was a test harness that omitted FolderReady delivery; capture now includes that event. Test-only corrections distinguish initial menu focus from ArrowDown and select the thumbnail rather than a same-named tab in the GPU fixture. Five opt-ins were ignored in the broad affected run; the newly required recycle was then explicitly passed, leaving four unrelated opt-ins unverified. Passing unchanged controls were retained after focused corrections; this is a combined checkpoint, not a new full-workspace aggregate. App all-target Release Clippy, fmt/diff/local-link checks and alternate normal Release pass; Clippy's initial test-only clone warning was corrected. Initial/final logs and source/artifact/readback hashes: `target/tmp/thumbnail-menu-results.json`; launcher: `target/release/start-thumbnail-menu.cmd`. Copy/Reveal controls capture exact intents without a real Explorer/clipboard-consumer trial; native mutation fixtures and offscreen input are not physical approval.

|---|---|
| Rust ownership/API boundaries, bounded media pipelines, shared rendering and documented handoff | Implemented under ARCHITECTURE: core/app forbid unsafe, native ownership stays in runtime, one D3D11 device, D3D11VA/software fallback and event-driven WASAPI Shared. Core/runtime regression evidence and Shell cancellation/lifetime cover ownership and teardown. Cargo.lock/toolchain stay pinned. The draft's CUDA/Exclusive/eframe/HDR alternatives are superseded or deferred, not additional parallel implementations. |
| Minimal image/video/audio UI and supplied visual references | All 16 exported reference PNGs were reviewed, including selection/grid, reading, filmstrip, drag guide, cards, palette, timeline/gain and Gallery. The later compact initial audio mode, native-caption decision and supplied replacement reading artwork supersede earlier drawings. Current media/picker GPU readbacks and layout/paint regressions are described below; this is not a claim of pixel-identical desktop composition. |
| File/Edit/View logo gesture and animation | `logo_menu.rs` uses the supplied angular sectors; menu dispatch, cancellation, held animation, keyboard/accessible entry and selected-arrow tint are verified. The audit found and repaired beveled logo corners in `chrome.rs`; the supplied SVG's cubic corners now remain round at button/drop-guide sizes. Current local verification is below. |
| Menus, searchable command palette, contextual/custom multistroke shortcuts and 4x4 per-media grid | `menu::tests::every_menu_command_has_exactly_one_leaf_location` covers registry reachability; `shortcuts`, `keyboard_settings`, `palette`, `grid` and overlay tests cover parsing, recording, contexts, physical cells, click/key dispatch and cancellation. Grid layout is text-configurable; its graphical editor is deferred. |
| Explorer-only mixed-media ordering, same-kind/all-kind navigation and filmstrip | Shell initial order includes the reported DateCreated descending folder and persisted-view native controls; `image_navigation`, `filmstrip` and saved-order tests cover traversal, clamping, background opens and selection. F5 reloads order; the owner withdrew independent reading-folder reversal. No natural-name order is substituted for an available Shell order. |
| External opens, audio-folder reuse, tabs, split/merge, drag destinations, Welcome/Gallery | Fresh media destinations, Thumbnail transfer, U06/U07/U08 and tab-drag checkpoints cover original-file ownership, retained edits/transport, new/background tabs, audio queue reuse, closed-tab restoration, last-tab handling and whole-client cross-window destinations. Native host/transfer tests supplement synthetic input; no physical drag approval is inferred. |
| Compact progress, image-folder/video hover previews, scrubbing and tab cards | `seekbar`, `image_scrub`, `image_tab_preview`, `tab_preview`, `preview_transport` and fade controls cover endpoints, preview/commit/cancel, source/tab guards, reading direction and background cards. Native session and first-frame readbacks are recorded in Thumbnail cards/V02; the original low-load main-view preview path is retained. |
| Status metadata/controls/notices, drop guide, fonts and app icon | U03/U04/U05, Clock labels and Status file details/settings record layout, clocks, fields, notices, system changes and input. `window_icon` verifies native resource loading; the tracked ICO is byte-identical to the supplied favicon. Figtree/Codicon provenance is retained. Drop-guide geometry/input tests pass after the logo correction. |
| Unsaved indicator, guards, source Save/Export as, Undo/Redo and metadata options | File operations, U07, E01 and source-backing/source-save regressions cover source replacement, retained originals and history, stale/cancel/failure guards, separate destinations and metadata transactions. Ctrl+S overwrites the source; derivative export remains separate. No new exit hang remains attached to this feature: the captured URLMON race has a diagnosed fix and native shutdown evidence. |
| Image rectangle selection, edge/ratio resizing, right-drag movement, selection fit, crop and aspect presets | I09/U11 and `selection`, `image_scroll`, image geometry and command-dispatch regressions cover square/aspect constraints, handles, edited-pixel bounds, gesture ownership, fit/clear and crop/Undo. The formerly excluded accessible-selection regression passes in the final baseline. |
| Quarter/free rotation, flips, wheel/key zoom, Actual/Fit/Cover and fullscreen | I01/I06/I07, Fine rotation, `rotation`, `video_rotation`, `image_scroll` and shortcut regressions cover transforms, composed free rotations, exact Fit stops and fullscreen/resize behavior. Plus/Equals defaults coexist and preserve custom keys; the owner confirms the physical key records Equals and works when assigned. Application chrome cannot zoom. |
| Image navigation aliases, Home/End and numbered/five-image jumps | I04 and `image_navigation::tests::jumps_*` cover Shell-filtered counting, bounds, real loading, reading and dirty guards. Deliberate draft variation: Ctrl+Left/Right keeps one-item same-kind navigation across media; Ctrl+Space/Backspace and Ctrl+5/Ctrl+Shift+5 provide five-image jumps. This preserves the existing consistent/customizable command contract instead of stealing the cross-media binding. |
| Edited-image/selection clipboard, resize/resample and display interpolation | I05 and clipboard/resize/sampling regressions cover snapshot pixels and alpha, transactional dimensions/filter controls, animation budgets and separate viewing interpolation. The explicit generated-animation GPU trial records 24 cases and 72 recovery/Undo/Redo comparisons. |
| Reading mode, 2-10 pages, first-spread count, axis/direction, pinned drag and retained focus | I02 and Reading folder order and icons cover read-only joined pages, bounded loading, direction-aware spatial navigation/seeks, active-drag axis switching, entry/cancel, leading-page retention and owner-supplied mirrored artwork. The newer instructions replace the draft's fixed horizontal adjustment and independent folder-reversal proposal. |
| Video/audio volume, pause, rates, seconds/frame steps, same-kind tracks and stationary 2x hold | A01/V04 and transport/volume/frame/seek tests cover listening versus saved gain, range/history guards, actual video PTS and audio small steps. Both hold-session controls now run explicitly and pass with real muted playback and no skips; prior rate/pause, edited bounds/history, focus, seek, tab and EOF behavior are checked. Owner-accepted frame-step performance is not reopened. |
| Timeline height, CTI/selection, range playback, Delete/Keep, stretch and relative gain | V03/A02 and time-selection/timeline tests cover gesture ordering, playback bounds, height/compact transitions, edited mappings, saved rate/gain and Undo. Later relative-gain semantics supersede absolute local adjustment: each 0-200% operation preserves existing ratios; cumulative gain is not capped, non-finite results are rejected. Waveform release/refinement retains the held geometry. |
| Video selection/crop/rotation/flip/resize with editing guard; audio repeat/shuffle | V05, video selection/raster/resize tests and audio playback/queue controls cover editing-only operations, display transforms, same-device rendering, end behavior and repeat/shuffle. Audio starts compact by later instruction, with timeline editing explicitly available. |
| Audio-only output, peak normalization, channel conversion, image/video export and requested quality/PNG fixes | E01/E02/E03/V07 record source protection, audio/channel/metadata output, generated decoded-output comparisons, animation fidelity and AV1 high-depth quality. The supplied HEVC midpoint PNG was compared at full depth after repairing packed RGB conversion. High-depth quality/chroma/orientation controls and the owner's selected lossy-quality policy are retained. LUFS/true-peak and compatible native formats are covered by the newer follow-up checkpoints above; full HDR remains design-only and ICC display retains its speed gate. |

### Original UI reference check (2026-09-19)

Reviewed the original image/video/audio concept exports and current menu/shortcut routing. `menu::tests::every_menu_command_has_exactly_one_leaf_location` compares menu leaves to the full command registry (cycle commands have explicit choice submenus); image jump/aspect/free-rotation dispatch and contextual shortcut/custom-prefix controls are present in the last completed workspace log. This is command reachability evidence, not every interaction's completion audit.

The renamed `ui_reference_tests::media_reference_layouts_reach_the_gpu` now covers image plus compact/editing audio and video: **one explicit opt-in passes, 15 hardware D3D11 full-client readbacks at 100/125/200%**. Generated muted WAV/constant-color MPEG-4 inputs open real paused sessions; the video decoder's first frame is advanced and drawn below the production UI. Pixel checks cover video/image visibility between toolbar/status, reserved timeline height, visible audio rows and compact played/unplayed ranges. Shell state, clock/durations and waveform remain seeded, so this does not certify actual clock/seek/waveform accuracy. Representative video and picker readbacks were visually inspected; no new product defect was found. Preserve the later owner-approved compact initial audio mode and native-caption decisions rather than restoring superseded draft details.

Two existing opt-ins also pass on this source: filmstrip **276 frames / 36 opens / 108 exact first/subsequent whole-surface comparisons / 12 held refreshes**, and keyboard/picker **18 generated GPU readbacks** across list/search/commands/files/folders/edit at three densities. Total: **three explicit native tests, zero failures/skips**. App all-target Release Clippy and fmt/diff pass. The initial video fixture patch had a compile-time branch-placement error; it was corrected before the final test. These are hidden generated-surface checks, not physical input, native captions, desktop compositor capture or all possible overlay states. Product behavior is unchanged; no new Release or full-suite aggregate is claimed. Current media/picker readbacks and hashed evidence: `target/tmp/media-reference-render/`, `target/tmp/overlay-reference-render/`, `target/tmp/media-reference-results.json`. Earlier image/audio-only evidence remains associated with `ae48572`.

Final local correction: the original SVG corners were approximated by two straight diagonals. The new geometric regression first fails at **0.187 physical pixels** deviation for the 16-point/100% logo. `chrome::paint_logo` now flattens the supplied cubic corners at a **0.05-physical-pixel tolerance**, joining the arms in one path; hit bounds, shaft animation and tint are unchanged. **17 logo/menu tests and two drop-guide tests pass**, including button/48-point guide sizes at 100/125/200%. The explicit media GPU control passes again with **15 new readbacks**; its 200% image surface was visually inspected. Both generated muted audio/video hold-session controls also pass explicitly, with PASS markers and no SKIP. Total current checks: **22 passed / zero failed / zero skipped**. App all-target Release Clippy, fmt/diff and alternate normal Release pass; the latter has only the expected `-o` output-name warnings. Review launcher: `target/release/start-logo-curves.cmd`. Logs, source/EXE/readback hashes and the deliberately failing pre-fix test are indexed by `target/tmp/concept-completion-results.json`.

The final baseline workspace remains **1,381 passed / zero failed / 115 ignored / one owner-deferred picker exclusion**, not a new aggregate after this local rendering correction. All 13 archived Shell-shutdown/media-reference logs and 66 previous readback artifacts were hash-verified during this audit. No runtime/core/dependency code changed in this continuation. The newly affected rendering paths have the checks above; ignored opt-ins are not promoted to passes. The old process-exit archives remain diagnostic history; the later Shell cancellation/lifetime checkpoint supersedes their unresolved-cause status for the captured URLMON race.

### Performance-goal completion audit

| Requirement | Current implementation and completion evidence |
|---|---|
| Still-image loading/display/switching, including repeats | Retained folder index, original-resolution decoding, bounded directional prefetch, presentation-gated accepted steps. Formal owner-operated `.2Ds` Fit trial: 303 inputs/303 targets/304 originals; ordered 100th transition 3743.588ms versus IrfanView sampled-ready upper bound4847.974ms. All shared originals retain dimensions; no low-resolution substitution. The owner confirmed Release resolves the severe delay. Later image changes are display-only preview paths; the original-decoder/held-navigation evidence is preserved. Not every input, cold start or GDI/scanout superiority. |
| Tab/recent/filmstrip and image/reading/video hover previews | Current consumers retain loaded original/reading pixels, shared RGBA/cache results and sheet UVs; ready/disk previews precede generation, tab completions wake idle native windows. Verified native redraw, 19 post-WIC app-consumer tests and runtime fidelity controls. Real mixed JPEG PreviewLoader batch414→344ms; indexed video-sheet decoding~1037→140ms on the long-GOP fixture. Raw per-path evidence and rejected alternatives are retained below; these are improvements, not a claim that every hover has a new physical-input comparison. |
| Heavy-video second seeking, including rapid replacement | Software preroll deferral reduces actual-worker late-GOP latency370-440→134-140ms with exact selected frames and terminal/cancellation guards. Two real hardware references:400 scheduled forward/reverse seeks include muted/paused WASAPI setup, reject stale targets and reach independently verified final PTS. Synchronous calls can still reach39.4ms; no physical-freeze-resolution claim or speculative persistent decoder. |
| Lower-priority waveform loading without sacrificing foreground work | Native overview/selected-stream discard and exact batched detail aggregation are adopted, with earlier measured gains. New 15-minute PCM plus image/thumbnail real-worker overlap trial passes all48 originals,48 thumbnails and four waveform outputs exactly. No substantial foreground blocking reproduced; priority gates, extra workers and progressive decode were not added without demonstrated benefit. This bounded warm-fixture result is not an all-device contention guarantee. |
| Adapt useful IrfanView mechanisms while retaining accuracy and simplicity | Rechecked the supplied analysis's2026-09-15 revision: reuse enumeration, distinguish original/thumbnail resolution, avoid needless pixel copies and reduce JPEG previews early. Current bounded ownership, shared ready pixels and guarded reduced-WIC thumbnails implement the useful principles; direct-PNG output and two-stage GDI prototypes were rejected/deferred where evidence did not justify them. Original/full-edit/export quality paths, one-device design, pinned dependencies and cache bounds remain unchanged by the final video/waveform checkpoints. |
| Proportional verification and handoff | Existing measured EXE/PDB/raw archives remain paired; current production video passed the full426-test Release runtime aggregate and normal app build. Later changes are test-only and pass their explicit opt-ins plus runtime all-target Release Clippy/fmt/diff. Skips, older aggregates and observer/GPU/input limitations remain labeled; no new full-workspace, installed-release or publication certification is implied. |

Publication, signing, and release recertification are deferred. An eventual assisted per-user installer is the accepted format, but not authorization to publish now. Windows 11 x64 is now the supported product/test target. Windows 10 compatibility fallbacks may remain, but Windows 10 is excluded from support and launch validation; do not install it or schedule its certification. The owner permits visible-window interaction and clipboard use for scoped work, not unrelated writes or input.

## Handoff

- Native caption work retains OS-drawn controls; U01 owns current caption evidence and remaining geometry/interaction issues. The owner identified the button-height change as Maximize, not fullscreen, and has authorized only the reversible existing-DWM border trial, not an AppWindow/SDK prototype. The prior hidden/minimized bounds fallback and visible multi-monitor evidence remain in Git; do not replace actual button bounds with a guessed fixed width.
- Accepted prefetch policy: The owner-approved **384 MiB decoded cache, eight neighbors in the current direction and one opposite** remains unchanged, with queued-step priority, 30 decoded entries, ten-path batches, 256 MiB texture cache and reading-spread selection. Bounded paired decoding is now enabled in normal construction; current adoption checks and measurements are in the Paired original-decode concurrency row. Earlier capacity/directional comparisons remain below and in Git. Installed executables were not replaced. Physical navigation/IrfanView comparisons, outliers and broader UI/feature review remain separate; the saved Shell-order report and captured URLMON exit race are resolved in Shell initial order and Shell cancellation/lifetime; do not repeat unchanged capacity or steady-direction trials.
- Previous resize checkpoint (`087b9c7`): disable implicit DXGI stretch between window resize and redraw; standalone/shared-caption descriptors, four-size presentation and opaque background pass. WARP/hardware fixed-left text pixels match byte-for-byte at widths 624–680 and densities 1/1.25/1.5/2; logical origins were already stable. Shared video/UI surface recreation and native caption image/video recovery preserve pixels/state with zero playback CPU transfers. Full workspace **912 passed / 43 ignored / two known exclusions**, vendor **5**. The native test runs in a subprocess because winit allows only one event loop per process. Not proof that the owner's physical right-edge shimmer is resolved; exposed-edge appearance and native caption geometry remain open.
- Album Artist investigation: audio/PNG already expose it. The [Adobe Dynamic Media schema](https://developer.adobe.com/xmp/docs/xmp-namespaces/xmp-dm/) defines artist, not a distinct Album Artist property. Do not silently map AlbumArtist to xmpDM:artist or invent a standard property; JPEG/WebP retain their nine-field contract pending a justified interoperable mapping. Further descriptive-XMP retention and broader metadata transfer remain open; omission of unknown technical XMP after raster edits is intentional.
- No build or owned media job was left running at that checkpoint. No distribution work should be resumed merely because old logs list it as next.
- Manual input is now demonstrated for the independent control and the navigation trial below. Other physical-operation audits remain open; automated delivery has not recovered. Do not bypass supported tools through private injection or relabel synthetic egui input as OS verification.
- Build setup and alternate FFmpeg prefixes belong in DEVELOPMENT/environment, not machine-specific paths here.

## Evidence worth reusing

Keep results that prevent redoing an investigation. Find exact test names/conditions in the cited commit or the historical lookup described in DEVELOPMENT. Do not rerun old long benchmarks without a relevant change or new question.

IrfanView decoder-call instrumentation (2026-09-15): both sides now use fresh processes, the same System32 DbgEng/QPC boundary timing and disabled debug heap. The new `decode-call` example invokes towavue's production static decoder without warm-up; the hash-matched IrfanView 4.73 probe matches the requested path at PNG RVA `0x5fa20` and times through its return address. Skip its internal 304×16 toolbar PNG. Warm-file I/T/T/I batches, three samples per batch, report these median ranges:

| PNG case | IrfanView ms | towavue ms |
|---|---|---|
| Generated RGB 32×24 | 0.303–0.305 | 0.407–0.421 |
| Generated RGB 502×316 | 1.632–1.729 | 1.248–1.274 |
| Generated RGB 4096×2304 | 82.704–82.930 | 45.074–45.321 |
| Reference largest RGB8, 4152×3008 | 84.805–84.821 | 34.171–34.194 |
| Reference largest RGBA8, 8706×5949 | 666.718–667.696 | 210.678–212.311 |

Every sample has identical full RGBA SHA-256 across the two decoders; hashes are computed after timing by normalizing IrfanView's bottom-up BGR(A) DIB. Source hashes/mtimes remain unchanged. Reference selection inspects PNG headers only (13 RGB8 and 57 RGBA8 candidates); no image appearance, screenshot or input injection. The Release example build, three ordinary generated-PNG invocations, example Clippy, fmt and diff checks pass; no production-library path changed. No compiler overlapped timing. This replaces the earlier asymmetric warm-runtime/fresh-Irfan comparison. **Decoder evidence only, not goal completion:** small-image debugger overhead is significant; IrfanView's format sniffing/internal-resource warm-up precedes its timed PNG call, whereas towavue includes format selection and orientation. Neither includes color-image preparation, display resampling, GPU/GDI transfer, compositor presentation, physical navigation or OS-cold I/O. Other desktop processes are uncontrolled. JPEG findings follow below; broader source/format and post-decode display evidence remain open. Do not repeat these unchanged large-PNG calls. The local experimental driver, exact results and scripts remain in `irfan-info-probe-e7e32c4ee1ed4ee598c39678bb015fdf`; no installed executable was changed and no owned process remains. LLDB step-out/script/resume and `/info` did not produce usable timing; do not retry them unchanged.

JPEG comparison after exact-memory-reader specialization: fresh-process DbgEng, unchanged heap/timing/source-matching method (Irfan JPEG entry RVA `0x58db0`), I/T/T/I batches of three. Median ms: generated 4096×2304 **21.118/17.332/17.614/20.861**; largest compressed references, 4961×3508 **121.473/124.207/124.267/121.360**, 2000×4000 **74.311/80.015/79.866/74.083**, 5000×6000 **105.078/90.048/90.311/105.196**. Towavue formerly needed ~150/99/106 ms on those references (`d70591d`); it still trails on the first two, so this is not goal completion. Source hashes/mtimes, dimensions and each decoder's repeated output are stable; cross-decoder RGBA hashes still differ on all four cases. Towavue's own pixel equivalence is independently checked below, not inferred from IrfanView. New decoder-example SHA-256 `BE7C3013F2EEE3354910D79237010CA2C4285B3D45A425ECC983B19880F9AFC2`; no compiler overlap, image appearance, cold-state, GPU/presentation or installed-app update.

JPEG/WIC decision after the owner's delegated review: **retain the current decoder; do not add a partial dispatcher on present evidence.** The analysis revision at 13:33 (57,372 bytes, sections 8.8–8.12 and 11.5) identifies IrfanView's WIC branch as CMYK/YCCK with no retained usable ICC, not a size-based ordinary-JPEG optimization. Its normal RGB/YCbCr decoder retains full resolution; “same quality” does not promise cross-backend pixel identity. It neither compares backend output nor defines a rounding tolerance. A legitimate bounded JPEG reconstruction difference need not automatically disqualify a faster backend, but observed differences alone do not establish correctness or a universal bound. CMYK/profile conversion differences are a separate color-policy question, not small rounding. Preserve the existing full-resolution/orientation/editing contract; no new color-management claim.

Rebuilt `jpeg-decoder-cost` against `217982b`; old **12–25% WIC gains are superseded**, not evidence for today's dispatcher. Untimed coding metadata now reports source bytes, SOF, colorspace, sampling and retained ICC length. Warm current/WIC/WIC/current, five samples per batch, including a fresh WIC thread/COM/factory/teardown: 12 references selected across compressed-size ranks 1/2/3/4/8/16/32/64/128/232/348/461. Eight baseline YCbCr sources all have no chroma subsampling (`None`, max factors 1×1), yet benefits reverse: rank 2 **80.697/74.050/73.528/81.671 ms**, rank 3 **90.778/98.198/95.206/90.745**, rank 8 **58.959/61.433/62.813/58.743**, rank 16 **20.909/18.486/18.757/20.712**. Four progressive sources are **~1.6–2.8× slower** with WIC. RGB maximum differences span **3–5/255**; 3/255 was not a universal ceiling. Sources with and without ICC occur on both sides of the speed comparison; profile presence is not a demonstrated routing rule. All 240 reference decodes preserve geometry/opaque alpha and repeat exactly within each backend; source hashes/mtimes are unchanged. An additional generated control passes, but its relative speed varies between batches, not a robust gain. The initial wrapper completed eleven cases then rejected its mistaken rank-463 assumption (461 direct-folder JPEGs); the actual last case passed separately and selection now uses the enumerated count. No Rust/native failure or unchanged-case rerun. Retain the earlier nine owned Pillow controls (`d70591d`): simple RGB/grayscale/progressive regressions and CMYK differences up to **111/255**, without inferring which CMYK conversion is colorimetrically correct. Example Release build, Clippy/fmt/diff pass; no production change, compiler overlap, private pixels/appearance, GPU/presentation, OS-cold, memory or installed-app evidence. Revisit WIC only with a justified eligible class and orientation/corruption/cancellation/budget/COM evidence, not another permission question or a threshold fitted to a few files. Exact scripts/results remain with the local probe above.

Rejected direct APNG pipeline: `apng_edited_export_reports_sequence_cost_and_pixel_digest`, Release, generated warm files, no concurrent build/test during measurement; three samples per case, baseline→direct Balanced→direct Fast→restored baseline. Median export ms for 240×640×360: **1072.640 / 5868.660 / 3285.575 / 1097.317**; 48×1920×1080: **2165.680 / 11073.108 / 6324.022 / 2174.173**. Output bytes respectively **12212490 / 12249500 / 14127468 / 12212490** and **23676439 / 23764852 / 24351244 / 23676439**. All runs retain dimensions, frame count and 1/30 delays, with matching decoded-RGBA CRC32 digests 61239103 / 8aeea9e3; small edited-frame/metadata/poster regressions passed on the trial. This rejects that whole sequential pipeline, not proof that intermediate I/O is free or that compression alone caused the regression: threading, filtering and encoding changed together. Timing includes export/probe/staging/publication, excludes fixture creation and readback; no OS-cold, physical UI, all-media or peak-memory claim. Baseline exe/PDB is retained locally under tvbuild/towavue-apng-edit-benchmark-20260913. That trial's production changes were not retained.

Piped APNG input: same Release benchmark, generated warm files, no concurrent build/test, staged→piped→piped→staged using the retained baseline executable. Three-sample median export ms: 240×640×360 **1079.006 / 852.780 / 833.492 / 1088.890**; 48×1920×1080 **2163.626 / 1685.971 / 1711.526 / 2190.402**. Pipe cancellation kills/reaps the child before joining workers; producer errors reject otherwise successful prefix output.

Reflection removes the 782-frame quiet tail on the one-second tone, but a 2400-frame input with 600 silent frames at 0.25x produces RMS **0.105726** in its final output quarter (zero context: **0**), with no trailing quiet samples instead of 3213. Evaluate a different boundary-aware time-stretch strategy against intentional silence as well as tone coverage, shared playback/export and exact joins; no tail synthesis or dependency change has been accepted. Existing finite context still prevents short input disappearing entirely. Five normal tempo tests plus the reflection diagnostic pass; no new listening, arbitrary-signal, ordinary-chain or universal pitch-quality evidence |

Background primary drags, including the inset gutters, swipe horizontally; thumbnails and the actual scrollbar retain their own input. Apply ordered motion once across discarded passes. Release momentum uses constant deceleration in logical points: speeds below 120 points/s have no tail, release speed is capped at 4,000 points/s and deceleration is 4,000 points/s squared (at most one second / 2,000 points). ### Owner-operated rapid Fit navigation

Verification-only hooks capture four navigation keys (code, press/release, repeat, synthetic flag) before consumption; navigation/target selection; queue push/full/pop/restore and blocked cancellation; loader cache/wait/foreground/prefetch outcomes; UI completion/acceptance/texture preparation; frame/UI/render/Present boundaries, successful original submissions and geometry; and Shell request/completion/application. Records retain QPC time, source token and the relevant generation; UI events include queue depth and loading/awaiting/error/reading/folder/held state. Loader generation is mailbox generation, not necessarily the generation that started an overlapping decoder: correlate source/start/return/outcome instead of assigning old work to the newest UI target. Instrumentation alone does not change navigation/cache policy; intentional held-repeat folding now uses QueueCancelled reason 3 (release) or 4 (lag/capacity), separate from blocked-state reasons 1/2.

The local `burst-fit-20260915` observer reads only its owned child without stops, screenshots, pixel reads or input injection. It retains Raw Input only for Right/Left/Space/Backspace while that child is foreground; no device IDs/text/other-app input. QPC is observer dispatch time, not hardware arrival; millisecond message times and clock anchors are retained. IrfanView 4.73 is hash-pinned, uses an isolated copy of owner settings (FitWindowOption 2, UseResample 1, UseZoomResample 0), and reads only index/path-token/DIB headers. Its main-DIB null→ready transitions are sampled, **not GDI completion**. The `/display` argument unexpectedly selected 100%; it was rejected and only the isolated INI window dimensions were changed. Generated controls verify the resulting comparable Fit area.

### Ready images in additional launch windows

A normal CLI launch into a running host creates another HWND, sharing its D3D11 device but not its Application/loader. The subsequent **minimal-stop 1:1 control** uses the same initial debugger-loader boundary and one selected stop at original submission in each app. IrfanView's official `/display` option sets 100% zoom; hash-pinned post-GDI call sites validate successful full-height transfer, the primary DIB and image-child HWND. towavue's verification-only actual-size option and display geometry validate the unedited original's 1:1 scale. All 24 owned debug handles signaled after cleanup; source hashes/mtime and geometry remain unchanged. This is an instrumented startup deficit, **not a native speed ratio or full goal result**. [WaitForEvent also processes other debugger events](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/dbgeng/nf-dbgeng-idebugcontrol-waitforevent); one selected stop does not eliminate debugger overhead.

### JPEG input buffering: rejected prototype

Following the analysis's streaming-stdio mechanism, compare full compressed-file reads with 8 KiB/64 KiB buffered input using the same strict zune RGBA decode. A specialized buffered exact-read path preserves partial output/position; a second prototype retains bytes across local seeks and caches logical position. Neither is adopted. Release, warm files, full/8K/64K/64K/8K/full, five samples per batch; generated 4096×2304 and compressed-size reference ranks 1/2/3/128. Ordinary buffered seeks regress every case (rank 1: full **126–136 ms**, 8K **239–260**, 64K **298–319**). Buffered local seeks remove much of that overhead but still do not justify replacing the current reader:

| Input | Full file ms | Buffered 8 KiB ms | Buffered 64 KiB ms |
|---|---|---|---|
| Generated 4096×2304 | 12.821–12.992 | 12.795–12.849 | 12.743–12.841 |
| Rank 1, 4961×3508 | 118.972–120.110 | 126.646–128.014 | 123.721–123.829 |
| Rank 2, 2000×4000 | 75.088–75.675 | 81.806–81.817 | 78.942–79.270 |
| Rank 3, 5000×6000 | 88.223–88.440 | 89.322–90.600 | 87.721–88.203 |
| Rank 128, progressive 1830×2048 | 24.723–25.181 | 31.869–32.838 | 31.713–32.652 |

Both prototypes pass all 300 timed full-pixel comparisons and source-byte/mtime checks without displaying/writing private pixels. Five ordinary JPEG/reader tests pass on the final prototype; the nine-fixture Pillow control passed before adding its test-only positioned reader. File open/read/decode/drop are timed; EXIF transformation, damaged-input fallback, presentation/GPU, physical input, OS-cold and process-memory measurements are not. Compiler work did not overlap timing; other desktop processes were uncontrolled. The prototype's Clippy check rejects twelve test-only `unwrap()` calls; it was discarded, not certified for adoption. All source changes were removed and the worktree's product code matches `26c8f0d`; no installed app changed. Exact trial patch/results are retained beside the local Irfan probe as `JPEG-STREAMING-TRIAL.patch` / `JPEG-STREAMING-RESULTS.txt`. The Release runtime test binary was subsequently rebuilt from current source for the 39 passing image-loader checks above; it no longer contains this rejected trial. Do not repeat these input variants unchanged. Prioritize post-decode display preparation and a comparable actual-app presentation boundary next, rather than another isolated JPEG backend substitution; the full goal remains unproven.
